- **impeccable-adapt**: Recontextualize an existing web design for a different screen size, device, platform, or use case. Use when adapting rather than extending a design - the trap is treating adaptation as scaling, when the real job is rethinking the experience for the new context. Covers assessing what breaks, phone-to-tablet restructuring, orientation and foldables, platform-to-platform idiom translation, and verifying custom controls under real touch input. ([JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit](https://github.com/JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit/tree/HEAD/upstream/impeccable-adapt) · ★4)
- **impeccable-adapt**: Impeccable adapt UI quality skill. Use when auditing or improving web interface implementation with the adapt workflow. ([KAYANSTOR/kayan-soft-website](https://github.com/KAYANSTOR/kayan-soft-website/tree/HEAD/.agent/skills/03-UI-UX-Design/impeccable/adapt))
- **impeccable-adapt-native**: Recontextualize an existing native design (iOS / Android / adaptive) for a different device class, orientation, platform, or origin. Use when adapting a native app, where the trap is treating adaptation as scaling. Covers size classes, window size classes, posture and hinge handling on foldables, and translating idioms rather than transplanting them between iOS and Android. ([JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit](https://github.com/JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit/tree/HEAD/upstream/impeccable-adapt-native) · ★4)
- **impeccable-android**: Android platform conventions for native apps built with Jetpack Compose, Android Views, React Native, Expo, or Flutter. Use when building or reviewing Android UI. Covers Material Design 3 as the governing rulebook, expressing brand through Material theming, the Android slop test for off-spec components, and the OS guarantees iOS hardware still owes. ([JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit](https://github.com/JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit/tree/HEAD/upstream/impeccable-android) · ★4)
- **impeccable-animate**: Review a feature and enhance it with purposeful animations, micro-interactions, and motion effects that improve usability and delight. ([LineLuLan/line-harness](https://github.com/LineLuLan/line-harness/tree/HEAD/claude/skills/impeccable-animate) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **impeccable-animate**: Add purposeful motion to a user interface. Use when a design needs animation that explains state, relationship, and hierarchy — not decoration, but motion that carries meaning. Covers motion thesis, material-by-meaning, timing and easing, implementation strategy, and accessibility. ([JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit](https://github.com/JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit/tree/HEAD/upstream/impeccable-animate) · ★4)
- **impeccable-anti-slop**: Use this skill when designing, auditing, refactoring, or polishing frontend UI/UX, CSS, and HTML to enforce high-craft design taste, prevent AI slop patterns (gradient clichés, nested cards, decorative side-tabs, flat typography, unverified states), and verify WCAG 2.2 accessibility and project DESIGN.md compliance. ([dosigner/physicalworks-design-harness](https://github.com/dosigner/physicalworks-design-harness/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/impeccable-anti-slop))
- **impeccable-asset-producer**: Produces clean reusable raster assets from approved Impeccable mock references without redesigning the direction. ([hafidzlvm/opencode](https://github.com/hafidzlvm/opencode/tree/HEAD/skills/impeccable-asset-producer) · ★1)
- **impeccable-audit**: Audit Mikka interface craft and UX resilience using an adapted Impeccable quality lens. Use for visual hierarchy, density, touch, motion, typography, focus and responsive correctness; never introduce a competing design system or modify paid UI internals. ([alexj11324/Mikka](https://github.com/alexj11324/Mikka/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/impeccable-audit) · ★88)
- **impeccable-audit**: Perform comprehensive audit of interface quality across accessibility, performance, theming, and responsive design. Generates detailed report of issues with severity ratings and recommendations. ([LineLuLan/line-harness](https://github.com/LineLuLan/line-harness/tree/HEAD/claude/skills/impeccable-audit) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **impeccable-audit**: Run systematic technical quality checks on a web interface and generate a scored report without fixing anything. Use when auditing implementation quality rather than critiquing design taste. Scores five dimensions 0-4 - accessibility, performance, responsive behavior, code quality, and theming - and documents findings for other procedures to address. ([JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit](https://github.com/JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit/tree/HEAD/upstream/impeccable-audit) · ★4)
- **impeccable-audit**: Impeccable audit UI quality skill. Use when auditing or improving web interface implementation with the audit workflow. ([KAYANSTOR/kayan-soft-website](https://github.com/KAYANSTOR/kayan-soft-website/tree/HEAD/.agent/skills/03-UI-UX-Design/impeccable/audit))
- **impeccable-audit**: A design quality workflow ported from the community `impeccable` skill (`pbakaus/impeccable`). Where `anti-slop` is purely a detector library, this skill adds impeccable's actual command structure: a one-time `init` that captures design context, plus `critique` (UX review) and `polish` (shipping-readiness) passes that sit on either side of the detector-based `audit`. ([rahlplx/project](https://github.com/rahlplx/project/tree/HEAD/skills/design/impeccable-audit))
- **impeccable-audit-native**: Run systematic technical quality checks on a native app (iOS / Android / adaptive) and generate a scored report without fixing anything. Use when auditing native implementation quality from source (SwiftUI, UIKit, Compose, React Native, Flutter). Scores five dimensions 0-4 against platform conventions, including VoiceOver and TalkBack accessibility. ([JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit](https://github.com/JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit/tree/HEAD/upstream/impeccable-audit-native) · ★4)
- **impeccable-bolder**: Amplify a section within an existing design system to make it bolder and more confident. Use when a specific section reads flat or timid compared to its neighbors, and needs to be raised to the expressive level the rest of the page already reaches — without rebuilding anything outside the named target. ([JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit](https://github.com/JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit/tree/HEAD/upstream/impeccable-bolder) · ★4)
- **impeccable-bridge**: Trigger: UI design direction, visual craft, design review, critique, audit, polish, build, design routing, design evaluation of an existing product, /akili-audit design. The AKILI layer over the vendored Impeccable design skill: skill source, engine probe and consent gate, phase map, design source of truth, detector gate (scan matrix, first-visit pass, exit codes), Design Waivers, privacy, and fallback to ui-ux-pro-max + frontend-design when the skill source is missing. ([JuankCadavid/akili-specs](https://github.com/JuankCadavid/akili-specs/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/impeccable-bridge) · ★12)
- **impeccable-bridge**: The comprehensive, end-to-end UI/UX design pass for production-grade frontend craft — use when the work is "make this genuinely excellent / bolder / more delightful / quieter", a full redesign, a deep design critique or audit, or live in-browser visual iteration, rather than one focused facet. This is a BRIDGE to the standalone `impeccable` skill (which does the heavy lifting); pro-design's focused skills (design-token, accessibility-audit, motion-system, typography-scale, shadcn-ui-composition) cover single facets, this routes to the full engine. Not for backend-only or non-UI tasks. ([jodybrewster/pro-dev-skillset](https://github.com/jodybrewster/pro-dev-skillset/tree/HEAD/plugins/pro-design/skills/impeccable-bridge))
- **impeccable-clarify**: Improve unclear UX copy, error messages, microcopy, labels, and instructions. Makes interfaces easier to understand and use. ([LineLuLan/line-harness](https://github.com/LineLuLan/line-harness/tree/HEAD/claude/skills/impeccable-clarify) · ★9 · 2 copies)
- **impeccable-clarify**: Improve UX copy, labels, error messages, and microcopy in a user interface. Use when interface text is unclear, ambiguous, jargon-heavy, or inconsistent — rewrite it so users understand what happened, what matters, and what to do next. ([JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit](https://github.com/JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit/tree/HEAD/upstream/impeccable-clarify) · ★4)
- **impeccable-clarify**: Impeccable clarify UI quality skill. Use when auditing or improving web interface implementation with the clarify workflow. ([KAYANSTOR/kayan-soft-website](https://github.com/KAYANSTOR/kayan-soft-website/tree/HEAD/.agent/skills/03-UI-UX-Design/impeccable/clarify))
- **impeccable-cn**: 用 Impeccable 方法审计、收敛和打磨 Tabora 的网页与插件界面。用户要求优化视觉、重做卡片、减少 AI 模板感、统一设计系统、改善布局层级或进行 UI polish/critique 时使用；适用于 Solid/TSX、StyleX、CSS 和独立 HTML 预览。 ([bijinfeng/tabora](https://github.com/bijinfeng/tabora/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/impeccable-cn) · ★2)
- **impeccable-colorize**: Introduce color into an interface as hierarchy, meaning, and atmosphere rather than decoration. Use when a surface is under-colored, when color needs to encode action, selection, status, or wayfinding, or when a palette needs to carry atmosphere. Preserves confirmed brand and semantic conventions instead of replacing the visual world. ([JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit](https://github.com/JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit/tree/HEAD/upstream/impeccable-colorize) · ★4)
- **impeccable-commands**: Use when the user invokes any Impeccable design command (/impeccable, /shape, /polish, /critique, /typeset, /colorize, /animate, /audit, /craft, /distill, /harden, /bolder, /quieter, /layout, /delight, /overdrive, /clarify, /adapt, /optimize, /extract, /document, /onboard, /init, /live) or asks to design, improve, or critique any frontend UI. Reads the full Impeccable skill installed at .cursor/skills/impeccable/SKILL.md. ([thehivegroup-ai/ai-development](https://github.com/thehivegroup-ai/ai-development/tree/HEAD/modules/stack-authorities/frontend/impeccable/skills/impeccable-commands))
- **impeccable-craft**: Opt-in shape-then-build flow for creating UI from scratch with maximum intentionality. 4-stage cycle: shape → refine → implement → polish. Invoke only when explicitly asked for "craft a UI" or "impeccable approach". ([RaNDoM6913/claude-code-superkit](https://github.com/RaNDoM6913/claude-code-superkit/tree/HEAD/packages/codex/skills/impeccable-craft) · ★3)
- **impeccable-craft**: Opt-in shape-then-build flow for creating a UI from scratch — gather brand context, commit to an aesthetic direction, then run the 4-stage craft cycle (Shape → Refine → Implement → Polish); invoke only when explicitly asked to craft a UI from scratch or use the impeccable approach. ([RaNDoM6913/claude-code-superkit](https://github.com/RaNDoM6913/claude-code-superkit/tree/HEAD/packages/frontend-ui/skills/impeccable-craft) · ★3)
- **impeccable-craft**: Quality floor and focused refinement passes for an existing web page's typography, colour, layout and finish. Use when asked to polish, typeset, colorize, re-layout, critique or fix the craft of a page that already has a direction, or before any UI edit to check the bans and verification checklist. Not for choosing a new look (use design-taste-frontend) or for 3D or app shells. ([aditiabhang/yummy-food](https://github.com/aditiabhang/yummy-food/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/impeccable-craft))
- **impeccable-craft-floor**: The non-negotiable quality floor and absolute bans to verify immediately before shipping any UI. Use as a final pre-ship checklist on a built result, not as an intention. Covers contrast ratios, real shadow depth, focus visibility, motion respect, and the mechanical defects that design review tends to miss. ([JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit](https://github.com/JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit/tree/HEAD/upstream/impeccable-craft-floor) · ★4)
- **impeccable-critique**: Impeccable critique UI quality skill. Use when auditing or improving web interface implementation with the critique workflow. ([KAYANSTOR/kayan-soft-website](https://github.com/KAYANSTOR/kayan-soft-website/tree/HEAD/.agent/skills/03-UI-UX-Design/impeccable/critique))
- **impeccable-delight**: Add personality and memorable moments to a product interface. ([JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit](https://github.com/JZKK720/cubecloud-skills-bundle-kit/tree/HEAD/upstream/impeccable-delight) · ★4)
- **impeccable-design**: AI design language for production-grade UI — 10 commands covering typography, color, motion, layout, and UX writing quality ([baekenough/oh-my-customcode](https://github.com/baekenough/oh-my-customcode/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/impeccable-design) · ★43 · 2 copies)
- **impeccable-design**: Use when designing, building, redesigning, critiquing, auditing, or polishing frontend UI. Upstream-synced Hermes adaptation of Impeccable (pbakaus/impeccable): design vocabulary, anti-pattern detector, and bounded-pass workflows for production-grade web interfaces without eating the context window. ([jcrabapple/hermes-skills](https://github.com/jcrabapple/hermes-skills/tree/HEAD/impeccable-design) · ★11)
